### [1] [When Does Low-Bit Quantization Preserve the Decisions of Vector Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09854)
*Wenxuan Xiao,Xu Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文从"比较"层面研究量化向量检索，提出无分布假设的比较翻转概率分解与协方差感知的尾部界，并给出 Vamana 邻居选择的确定性耦合定理，用"标准化精确边距"比全局秩相关更好地预测排序与剪枝翻转率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在某些向量表示上召回率高、在另一些上骤降，而平均失真与全局秩相关无法解释这一差异，因此需要从排序与图剪枝算法实际消耗的"比较"层面理解量化失败机制。

Method: 1) 无分布假设分解：比较翻转概率 ≤ 接近零的精确边距概率质量 + 校准残差的尾概率；2) 考虑共享查询或图节点残差间的依赖，导出协方差感知的二阶矩恒等式与联合MGF代理下的尾部界；3) 对冻结候选排列证明 Vamana 邻居选择的确定性耦合定理；4) 通过精确高斯 oracle 联系表示几何，证明对齐双线性模型中确定性幅度比特带来严格相关增益，并用稀有污染构造说明边缘高斯诊断不能推出所需残差尾部；5) 无解析假设时用留出块证书界定冻结量化规则的选择性失败风险。

Result: 在学习型、经典与合成嵌入上，标准化精确边距对留出排序与剪枝翻转率的预测显著优于全局秩相关；框架通过统一决策接口适用于坐标二值码、RaBitQ、Lucene BBQ 与乘积量化器。

Conclusion: 量化向量检索的可靠性应基于比较级别的边距与残差尾部来刻画，标准化精确边距是预测选择性失败的有效诊断量，该框架统一适用于多种主流量化方法。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization can achieve high recall on some vector representations and fail sharply on others, while average distortion and global rank correlation do not explain the difference. We study quantized vector search at the level of the comparisons consumed by ranking and graph-pruning algorithms. Our first result is a distribution-free decomposition: the probability that a comparison flips is bounded by the probability mass of exact margins near zero plus the tail probability of the calibrated residual. We then account for dependence between residuals that share a query or graph node, and derive covariance-aware second-moment identities and tail bounds under a joint MGF proxy. For a frozen candidate permutation, we prove a deterministic coupling theorem for Vamana neighbour selection: the approximate replay returns the exact neighbour list exactly when all candidate-level pruning actions agree on the frozen exact states. We connect these results to representation geometry through an exact Gaussian oracle, establish a strict correlation gain from a deterministic magnitude bit in an aligned bilinear model, and give a rare-contamination construction showing why marginal Gaussian diagnostics do not imply the required residual tails. When analytical assumptions are unavailable, a held-out block certificate bounds the selective failure risk of a frozen quantized rule. Across learned, classical, and synthetic embeddings, standardized exact margins predict held-out ranking and pruning flip rates substantially better than global rank correlation. The framework applies to coordinate binary codes, RaBitQ, Lucene BBQ, and product quantizers through a common decision interface.

</details>


### [2] [Contextual Utility of Quantization Moves in Extreme Low-Bit LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09867)
*Wenxuan Xiao,Xu Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文指出后训练量化中"量化移动"的效用是上下文相关的：需沿移动路径（中点）评估，并从演化中的量化状态逐步组合，据此改进局部选择并用精确端点束搜索获得稀疏且更优的更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化器依赖重建代理或当前状态的局部损失近似来选择有限码变化，但忽略了移动效用取决于执行该移动时所处的状态，导致重建最优码重选与加性组合方法失效。

Method: ①从"位移"角度，在移动中点评估梯度以捕捉当前状态线性化遗漏的曲率；②从"交互"角度，用二次伪布尔函数近似合法量化状态的穷举格，分析成对分量对Pareto前沿与评价泛函方向选择的影响；③在中点读数修正局部选择步骤，并用精确端点束搜索（从实际到达的状态出发）处理更大支撑集。

Result: 在Llama-3.2模型的冻结二比特移动上，中点评估预测精确端点损失变化方向显著优于当前状态梯度；二次伪布尔近似能很好拟合穷举格，但微小成对分量即可决定Pareto前沿并使不同评价泛函偏好相反方向；中点读数提升下游准确率与留出困惑度；精确端点束搜索找到的稀疏改动优于更大的一次性更新；干预后再对相同移动重新定价出现大量符号反转。

Conclusion: 量化效用在少数移动的粒度上是上下文相关的：可靠构造必须沿各自路径评估有限变化，并从不断演化的量化状态出发进行组合。

Abstract: Post-training quantizers select finite code changes using reconstruction proxies or local loss approximations, but the utility of a quantization move depends on the state through which it is executed. We identify two sources of this contextual dependence. First, the displacement of the move matters: evaluating the gradient at the move midpoint captures curvature accumulated along the move that a current-state linearization omits. Across frozen two-bit moves from Llama-3.2 models, midpoint evaluation predicts the direction of exact endpoint loss changes substantially more accurately than current-state gradients. Second, moves interact: exhaustive lattices of legal quantized states are well approximated by quadratic pseudo-Boolean functions, yet their small pairwise components can determine Pareto fronts and cause different evaluation functionals to prefer opposite directions. These effects explain failures of reconstruction-optimal code re-selection and additive composition. Reading each move at its own midpoint repairs the local selection step and improves downstream accuracy and held-out perplexity, while larger supports require evaluating exact endpoints from the state actually reached. Exact-endpoint beam search finds sparse changes that dominate much larger one-shot updates, and repricing the same moves after intervening changes produces widespread sign reversals. These results show that quantization utility is contextual at the granularity of a few moves: reliable construction must evaluate finite changes along their own paths and compose them from the evolving quantized state.

</details>


### [3] [Few Rows Tell Them Apart: Equivalence of Queries Mixing Set and Bag Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09978)
*Sara Cohen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文为有界SQL等价性检查器提供了可计算的理论界限：若两个查询在所有每关系至多含B个元组的数据库上一致，则它们等价，从而使有界搜索成为终止且完备的判定过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界SQL等价性检查器通过搜索有界大小的反例数据库来判定等价，但搜索为空并不能证明等价，缺乏理论保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，给出可计算的界限，使有界搜索在特定查询片段上成为完备的判定过程。

Method: 在combined-semantics框架下（刻画SQL中DISTINCT去重与保留重复计算对集合值关系的混合语义）进行分析。针对合取查询，证明在固定多重集宽度下存在线性于查询大小的界限；考虑声明键与无环外键对界限的影响；并将结果推广到带比较运算的若干查询类。

Result: 对合取查询，若两个查询不等价，则它们在一个每关系至多含2^w|Q|个元组的数据库上已经不一致，其中宽度w仅计查询实际读取的列，与多重集变量总数无关。声明键将界限缩小为2^{kw}|Q|（kw为键宽度）；无环外键不改变界限；结果可扩展到此前未被刻画等价性的带比较运算查询类。

Conclusion: 对于这些查询片段，有界搜索成为终止且完备的等价性判定过程，弥补了有界SQL等价性检查缺乏理论保证的缺陷。

Abstract: Bounded SQL equivalence checkers search for a counter-example database of bounded size, and a search that comes back empty proves nothing. We supply missing theory: computable bounds $B$ such that agreement on all databases with at most $B$ tuples per relation implies equivalence. We work in the combined-semantics framework, which captures SQL's mix of duplicate-eliminating (DISTINCT) and duplicate-preserving computation over set-valued relations. For conjunctive queries we prove a bound linear in the query size for fixed multiset width: inequivalent queries already disagree on a database with at most $2^w |Q|$ tuples, where the width $w$ counts only the columns the queries actually read, independently of the total number of multiset variables. Declared keys shrink the bound to $2^{kw} |Q|$ for the smaller key-width $kw$, acyclic foreign keys leave it unchanged, and the result extends to several classes of queries with comparisons, for which equivalence had not previously been characterized. For these fragments, bounded search becomes a terminating, complete decision procedure.

</details>

### [4] [OASIS: A Rubric-Based Multimodal Assessment Platform Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09180)
*Ameer H. Shakur,Shinyoung Kang,David Hein,Michael J. Holcomb,Huong-Tra Ngo,Aarash Zakeri,Minhan Park,Licheng Yi,Dhanush Jain,Hunter Schuler,Andrew R. Jamieson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OASIS是一个基于LLM的评分基础设施平台，支持视频/音频/文本的多模态量规评分，包含CLI与集成栈两条路径，已在UT Southwestern生产运行，处理超7000次会面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个artifact用LLM评分简单，但大规模部署评估需要会面管理、量规版本控制、模态感知执行、溯源捕获和人工复核。论文动机是提供系统级平台解决规模化评分的基础设施问题。

Method: OASIS结合独立的命令行接口与规范的Elephant+MAPLES集成栈，用于会面管理与多模态评分编排。支持托管API或通过Ollama/vLLM自托管开源权重模型。可选扩展SimRubrics（量规编写）和Wayfinder（对话智能体网关），共享认证接口。输入量规和录制的encounters，输出逐标准分数、证据与理由，并保留执行产物以供审计。

Result: 系统具备量规即程序编译、渐进式执行计划、内容可寻址评分身份、转写增强的多模态评分、显式复核状态、人类与自主智能体共享命令界面等特色。自2023年秋在UT Southwestern Medical Center生产运行，已处理超过7000次encounters。

Conclusion: 虽起源于医学教育，但架构与领域无关，可用于任何从记录或书面artifact评估结构化表现的场景。本文仅包含报告与项目信息，不含应用源码、二进制、安装材料、样本数据或带标签的软件发行版。

Abstract: OASIS (Open Assessment and Scoring Infrastructure Stack) is a systems platform for rubric-based grading of video, audio, and text with large language models. Scoring one artifact with an LLM is straightforward; deploying assessment at scale requires encounter management, rubric versioning, modality-aware execution, provenance capture, and human review. OASIS pairs a standalone command-line interface with a canonical integrated Elephant + MAPLES stack for encounter management and multimodal grading orchestration. Both paths can target hosted APIs or self-hosted open-weight models through Ollama and OpenAI-compatible endpoints such as vLLM. SimRubrics rubric authoring and the Wayfinder conversational agent gateway are optional extensions that use the same authenticated interfaces as human operators. Given a rubric and recorded encounters, OASIS produces per-criterion scores, evidence, and rationales, preserving execution artifacts for audit. Distinctive features include rubric-as-program compilation, progressive execution plans, content-addressable grading identity, transcript-augmented multimodal grading, explicit review state, and a shared command surface for humans and autonomous agents. Though developed in medical education, the architecture is domain-agnostic, applying wherever structured performance can be evaluated from recorded or written artifacts. In production at UT Southwestern Medical Center since Fall 2023, the platform has processed more than 7,000 encounters. This publication includes the report and project information, not application source, binaries, installation materials, sample data, or a tagged software release.

</details>


### [5] [Evaluating Enterprise Analytics Agents: An End-to-End, Trace-Backed Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09182)
*Teja Venkat Kolli,Sang Su Lee,Xueying Yan,Jessie Chen,Chi Cheng,Kartik Ravisankar,Shishir Dash,Vijay Anand Raghavan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向企业分析智能体的端到端评估方法，涵盖语义理解、执行质量与可靠性三大维度，通过带金标答案的问题库、重复运行与运行时轨迹进行分层、弃权感知评分，并在某大型在线市场内部智能体上以50题、300条轨迹做案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析智能体并非单纯的文本到SQL系统，它们需理解业务意图、选择指标定义、挑选数据源、执行工具并生成影响运营与财务决策的自然语言答案。仅评估最终答案会掩盖失败点：答案看似合理却可能用错数据源、遗漏必要分解、无依据地宣称因果，或在重复运行中改变表理解。因此需要端到端、基于轨迹的评估。

Method: 提出端到端评估方法，从三类行为族评分：语义理解、执行质量、可靠性。使用人工撰写金标答案的问题库、重复运行与运行时轨迹；每次运行先通过运行有效性检查，再获得分层且感知弃权的分数，并输入决策框架而非简单的发布门禁。

Result: 在大型在线市场的受控内部分析智能体上实例化，采用50个分析问题、两种匿名模型配置、每种三次随机重复，共300条轨迹。高能力配置将早期拒答率从73%降至0%，真实数据回答率从21%升至73%；但在16%的运行中耗尽工具轮次预算，77%的轨迹超出模式探索预算，并在50个问题中的41个上改变了表理解。在具有结构化金标答案的财务问题上，源表使用与升级有所改善，但两种配置的规范分解仍然薄弱。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对分析智能体的信任需要端到端、轨迹支撑的评估，而不能仅依赖SQL正确性或最终答案质量。

Abstract: Enterprise analytics agents are not only text-to-SQL systems. They interpret business intent and choose metric definitions. They select data sources, execute tools, inspect results, and produce natural-language answers. Those answers may influence operational, financial, or executive decisions. Grading final answers hides where these agents fail. A plausible answer can use the wrong source of truth. It can skip a required decomposition, claim causality without support, or change its table interpretation across repeated runs. We present an end-to-end evaluation methodology for analytics agents. The methodology grades agent behavior across three families: semantic understanding, execution quality, and reliability. Grading uses question banks with human-written golden answers, repeated runs, and runtime traces. Each run first passes a run-validity check, then receives tiered, abstention-aware scores that feed a decision framework rather than a release gate. We instantiate the methodology on a controlled internal analytics agent at a large online marketplace. The case study uses 50 analytics questions, two anonymous model configurations, and three randomized repetitions per configuration, yielding 300 traces. The higher-capability configuration reduced early refusal from 73% to 0% and increased real-data answers from 21% to 73%. However, it also exhausted the tool-round budget on 16% of runs. It overran the schema-exploration budget on 77% of traces. It changed its table interpretation on 41 of 50 questions. On finance questions with structured golden answers, source table use and escalation improved, but canonical decomposition remained weak in both configurations. These results show why trust in analytics agents requires end-to-end, trace-backed evaluation rather than SQL correctness or final answer quality alone.

</details>


### [6] [The Double Measurement Confound in Agent Benchmarks: De-Scaffolding, Ground-Truth Scoring, and Reliability Beyond the Mean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09218)
*Yonghong Zhang,Shadi Motaali,Vu Phong Dinh,Avin Piroutiniya,Jorge E. López de Vergara,Luis de Pedro,Ricardo Correia,Isabel M. Parra,Yong Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM智能体基准测试中的双重测量混淆（脚手架承担执行决策、评分标准偏离任务正确性），提出测量理论框架与审计-修复协议，并在ComtradeBench上验证其可将近乎平坦的排行榜转化为可靠性谱系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准测试被广泛用于比较LLM并指导部署决策，但只有当分数反映模型能力而非评测流程本身属性时才有意义。作者发现两个混淆：执行关键决策由固定脚手架而非模型完成；评分器使用的标准可能不反映任务正确性。因此需要一个能刻画"分数何时可作为模型能力证据"的理论框架及可操作的审计与修复方法。

Method: 提出测量理论框架以界定基准分数可解释为模型能力证据的条件，并实例化为"审计-修复"协议：(i) 将执行关键决策从脚手架转移给模型；(ii) 用带种子的真值评分替换基于形状的评估；(iii) 通过最坏情况与尾部风险指标报告均值之外的可靠性。在ComtradeBench上进行实验，并将审计应用于现有基准。

Result: 在ComtradeBench上，联合干预将原本近乎平坦的排行榜转变为能够同时区分平均性能与跨随机种子鲁棒性的可靠性谱系。对现有基准的审计显示：评分器有效性是基准特有的，而脚手架归属在所探查的各个场景中都是一个不受控的维度。

Conclusion: 基准分数应连同其脚手架水平、评分标准和可靠性画像一起被解读。该工作为LLM智能体更有效的评测提供了实用框架，提示未来基准报告需明确这些维度。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are increasingly used to compare large language models (LLMs) and guide deployment decisions, yet benchmark scores are meaningful only if they measure model capability rather than properties of the evaluation pipeline. We identify a double measurement confound: execution-critical decisions are performed by a fixed scaffold instead of the model, while the scorer evaluates outputs using criteria that may not reflect task correctness. We unify these issues within a measurement-theoretic framework that characterizes when benchmark scores can be interpreted as evidence of model capability, and instantiate it with an audit-and-repair protocol that (i) transfers execution-critical decisions from the scaffold to the model, (ii) replaces shape-based evaluation with seeded ground-truth scoring, and (iii) reports reliability beyond the mean through worst-case and tail-risk metrics. Experiments on ComtradeBench show that the joint intervention transforms a nearly flat leaderboard into a reliability spectrum that distinguishes both average performance and robustness across seeds. Applying the audit to existing benchmarks further shows that scorer validity is benchmark-specific, whereas scaffold ownership is an uncontrolled axis wherever we probed it. Our results suggest that benchmark scores should be interpreted together with their scaffolding level, scoring criterion, and reliability profile, providing a practical framework for more valid evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [7] [Introducing Consort: A Spec-First Agent Framework for Enforced, Test-Driven Development on Live Database Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09671)
*Kevin Hartman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将现有 spec-first、agent 驱动的代码开发框架按"工程纪律执行方式"划分为三种模式，并提出 Consort——一个通过 agent 无法绕过的确定性控制来强制执行纪律的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 2025 年以来 spec-first、agent 驱动框架的迅速流行，各主流框架（GitHub Spec Kit、obra/superpowers、BMAD、GSD 等）都在前期捕获意图上达成一致，因此区分它们的关键在于如何执行使 agent 代码保持整洁、正确、可维护的工程纪律。作者希望明确这一问题并给出更可靠的解决方案。

Method: 作者将纪律执行归纳为三种模式：1）说服式（提示词纪律，模型可能忽略）；2）前置结构式（强规格加可信构建）；3）不可编辑控制式（确定性编排器、人工审批门、不可变测试、必须在实时分支数据库上通过的绿色结果）。据此提出 Consort：一个 spec-first、测试驱动的 agent 框架，采用第三种模式，由确定性编排器驱动多个角色 agent，经过 spec-first 设计通道与基于实时数据库分支的测试驱动构建通道。

Result: 论文提出了 Consort 框架及其三模式分类法，并将其主张表述为一个预注册、可检验的假设；摘要中未给出实验性或实证结果，核心贡献是概念框架与框架设计。

Conclusion: 作者主张，在代码层面强制执行测试与门控能让 agent 编写的代码保持诚实与可验证；而其专门化角色（如同此前的人类角色）则使代码具备可维护性。这些主张被设定为预注册、可验证的假设，有待后续检验。

Abstract: When an agent writes code, the development framework becomes the control system for a non-deterministic worker. Spec-first, agent-driven frameworks have gained rapid traction since 2025; the installable ones, GitHub Spec Kit, obra/superpowers, BMAD, and GSD, and our own, all capture intent through a specification or durable planning artifacts. Since they agree on capturing intent up front, what separates them is how each enforces the engineering discipline that keeps agent-written code clean, correct, and maintainable. Every framework enforces that discipline somehow; they differ in how. We characterize three modes: enforcement by persuasion (prompt discipline the model may ignore), by front-loaded structure (strong specs, then a trusted build), and through controls the agent cannot edit (a deterministic orchestrator, human-approved gates, immutable tests, and a green result that must pass against a live, branched database). We introduce Consort, a spec-first, test-driven agent framework built on the third, enforcing that discipline through controls the agent runs inside but cannot bypass, in which a deterministic orchestrator drives separate role agents through a spec-first design lane and a test-driven build lane on a live database branch. We argue that enforcing the tests and gates in code keeps agent-written code honest and verifiable, while its specialized roles, like the human roles before them, are what make it maintainable, claims we frame as a pre-registered, testable hypothesis.

</details>


### [8] [Cost-Aware Post-Hoc Deferral Under Calibration and Shift: An Environmental AI Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09235)
*Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Danping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出EcoTrust框架，研究冻结分类器在延迟决策（自动执行vs人工复核）中置信度校准、类别不对称成本、复核者误差和分布外支撑的交互作用，发现在一个受控任务上更丰富的风险信号未必优于校准后的置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择延迟策略不只依赖对不确定样本的排序：置信度可能校准失当、错误成本不对等、复核者也会犯错，且部署数据可能超出校准支撑范围。作者希望系统研究这些因素的相互作用。

Method: 提出事后框架EcoTrust，使用六组错误风险估计器比较自动动作与人工复核，纳入类别不对称成本、复核者准确率，并可选加入支撑门控（support gate）。在Columbia River热应激测试台上评估，并与Chow置信度规则对比，扩展到12个现成后端和十个河流站点的迁移实验，采用配对年份-区块自助法。

Result: 学习到的估计器将错误排序的ROC AUC从0.869提升到0.889，但Chow置信度规则在分布内成本更低（每日0.416 vs 0.567）。在12个后端中，学习风险与校准匹配的类别感知置信度估计器各有六个优于原始Chow，配对自助法无法区分两者平均成本差异。迁移到十个站点时门控标记所有样本，退化为总是复核，仅在复核完美且无约束时于八个站点取得最低成本。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了一个受控任务上的决策边界：更丰富的风险信号不能可靠地超越校准后的置信度，检测到外推并不意味着可迁移的样本级排序。

Abstract: Choosing a deferral policy for a frozen classifier requires more than ranking uncertain cases: confidence may be miscalibrated, errors have unequal costs, reviewers can err, and deployment data can leave calibration support. We study these interactions through EcoTrust, a post-hoc framework that compares automatic action with review using a six-group error-risk estimator, class-asymmetric costs, reviewer accuracy, and an optional support gate. On a Columbia River thermal-stress testbed, the learned estimator improves error-ranking area under the receiver operating characteristic curve from 0.869 to 0.889, but Chow's confidence rule has lower in-distribution cost (0.416 versus 0.567 per day). Across 12 off-the-shelf backends, learned risk and a calibration-matched, class-aware confidence estimator each beat raw Chow on six; a paired year-block bootstrap does not resolve their mean cost difference. In transfer to ten river stations, the gate flags every case and becomes an always-review fallback, attaining the lowest cost on eight stations only when review is perfect and unconstrained. These results characterize decision boundaries on one controlled task: richer risk signals do not reliably improve on calibrated confidence, and detected extrapolation does not imply transferable case-level ranking.

</details>


### [9] [Talking to Itself While Coding: What Makes Comments Help Code Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09242)
*Dangfeng Pan,Zhensu Sun,Cenyuan Zhang,David Lo,Xiaoning Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评论之所以能提升代码生成，不是因为它们"是评论"，而是因为其承载了正确的解题内容，而这类内容靠提示词无法稳定激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在写代码时会生成自然语言评论，这些评论又成为后续生成代码的上下文，但究竟评论的哪些属性影响代码生成性能尚不清楚。

Method: 结合观察性分析与受控干预：在LiveCodeBench上考察评论频率与评论意图的预测作用；将强模型的注释块预填充给较弱的接收模型，以分离评论的表面形式与其传递的解题内容；并测试不同模型与提示词变体下的增益恢复情况。

Result: 评论频率与宽泛的评论意图均无法可靠预测pass@1；来自通过测试的源解的评论使接收模型pass@1平均提升17.2%；描述失败解的评论无稳定增益；针对不同问题的评论使pass@1下降20.8%；多数接收模型无法显著恢复外部评论带来的增益，最佳情况仅恢复24%。

Conclusion: 评论对代码生成的帮助并非源于评论形式本身，而在于其能提供正确的解题内容，且这种内容无法通过提示词可靠获得。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate natural-language comments while writing code, and these comments become part of the context used to generate the code that follows. However, it remains unclear which properties of comments affect code-generation performance. We study this question through observational analyses and controlled interventions. On LiveCodeBench, neither comment frequency nor broad comment intent reliably predicts pass@1. We then prefill weaker recipient models with comment blocks written by stronger source models, allowing us to separate comment surface form from the solution content they convey. Comments from source solutions that pass the tests raise recipient pass@1 by 17.2% on average. In contrast, comments describing failed solutions provide no reliable gain, while comments written for a different problem reduce pass@1 by 20.8%. Finally, across a wide range of models and prompt variants, most recipient models show no significant recovery of the external-comment gain, and the best case recovers only 24%. These results show that comments help code generation not merely because they are comments, but because they can provide correct solution content that prompting cannot reliably elicit.

</details>


### [10] [How effective are traditional test criteria at detecting bugs in large language models generated code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09315)
*Asma Hamidi,Michael Konstantinou,Renzo Degiovanni,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过5个LLM、4个基准的端到端实证研究，收集6000+错误程序实例，评估语句覆盖、分支覆盖和变异测试三种测试充分性准则对LLM生成代码中错误的检测效果，发现实际故障检测率接近零，测试预言（oracle）是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试充分性准则长期被用于评估和指导软件测试，但以往研究主要基于人类编写的程序、故障和测试。随着LLM被广泛用于代码生成，这些准则能否有效检测LLM引入的故障尚不清楚：LLM生成的代码、测试、错误模式可能与传统场景差异显著。作者希望实证检验在"代码与测试均由LLM自动生成"的端到端流程中，传统充分性准则的有效性与效率。

Method: 开展实证研究：选用5个LLM和4个基准，模拟代码与测试全部自动生成的端到端工作流，收集6000+错误程序实例。评估3种广泛使用的充分性准则——语句覆盖、分支覆盖和变异测试——在触发与检测故障方面的有效性和效率，并进一步考察提示感知（prompt-aware）oracle的作用。

Result: 主要发现：(1) LLM引入的大多数故障相对容易捕获；(2) 较难的故障很难被传统覆盖准则或变异准则触发；(3) 实际故障检测率极低，常接近零，原因是测试oracle无法捕获由生成测试前缀触发的错误行为，暴露了自动化测试生成的关键局限；(4) 提示感知oracle能提升故障检测，但整体效果有限，说明用户仍需人工推理测试断言；(5) 变异测试在触发和检测故障上仅略微优于传统覆盖准则，其在显著更高应用成本下是否值得存疑。

Conclusion: 传统测试充分性准则在LLM生成代码场景下效果受限，瓶颈主要在测试oracle而非覆盖率指标；变异测试相比覆盖准则优势有限而成本高昂。研究提示：需要更关注断言/oracle的有效性，用户仍需人工推理测试断言。

Abstract: Test adequacy criteria are widely used to evaluate and guide software testing. Although prior research has extensively examined these criteria using human-written programs, faults, and tests, the increasing adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation raises important questions about their effectiveness in detecting LLM-induced faults. To investigate this, we conduct an empirical study involving 5 LLMs and 4 benchmarks, simulating end-to-end workflows in which both code and tests are automatically generated. We collect 6,000+ faulty program instances and evaluate the effectiveness and efficiency of 3 widely used adequacy criteria: statement coverage, branch coverage, and mutation testing. Our findings reveal several key insights. First, most faults introduced by LLMs are relatively trivial to catch. Second, the challenging faults are difficult to trigger using either traditional coverage-based or mutation-based criteria. Third, actual fault detection rates remain extremely low, often near zero, because test oracles fail to capture faulty behavior triggered by the generated test prefixes, exposing a critical limitation of automated test generation. Fourth, prompt-aware oracles can improve fault detection, but their overall effectiveness remains limited, highlighting the need for users to manually reason about test assertions. We further observe that mutation testing only marginally outperforms traditional coverage criteria in both triggering and detecting faults, raising questions about whether its significantly higher application cost is justified in this context.

</details>


### [11] [The Vibe Shift in Software Engineering: Evaluating AI-Led Conversational Programming for Performance, Cognition, and Responsible Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09560)
*Sales G. Aribe,Louie Jay S. Labastida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了"氛围编程"（Vibe Coding）这一新兴的AI主导对话式编程范式，通过混合方法实验发现其显著提升了开发效率，但以可维护性下降和安全隐患增加为代价，并提出负责任采用的三支柱框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的自然语言交互编程范式兴起，亟需系统评估其在性能效率、认知影响和负责任采用方面的表现，并与传统编程及AI辅助编程环境进行对比。

Method: 采用混合方法设计，30名参与者（专业开发者与高级计算机专业学生）在三种实验条件下完成等价编程任务；定量数据用描述性统计和重复测量方差分析，定性数据用主题分析。

Result: 氛围编程显著提升开发效率，任务完成时间较传统编程减少27%、较AI辅助编程减少12%；但可维护性指标下降、安全漏洞增多；可用性SUS=71.4（良好），认知负荷NASA-TLX=55.5（中等），语法负担降低但语言推理增加；主题分析识别出信任校准、控制感缺失、认知适应和提示工程策略四个关键构念，其中控制感缺失与安全风险上升相关。SUS=71.4

Conclusion: 氛围编程能提升生产力，但需要批判性监督；研究提出三支柱负责任采用框架：人机能力混合整合、人工监督与透明问责、情境感知部署，认为其是软件开发中具有变革性但过渡性的范式。

Abstract: This study evaluates Vibe Coding, an emerging AI-led conversational programming paradigm that enables developers to generate software through natural-language interaction with large language models. Using a mixed-methods design, the study assessed performance efficiency, cognitive implications, and responsible adoption in comparison with traditional and AI-assisted coding environments. Thirty participants, including professional developers and advanced computing students, completed equivalent programming tasks under three experimental conditions. Quantitative data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and repeated-measures ANOVA, while qualitative data were examined through thematic analysis. Results show that vibe coding significantly improved development efficiency, reducing task completion time by 27% compared with traditional coding and 12% compared with AI-assisted coding. However, these gains were accompanied by lower maintainability indices and higher security vulnerabilities, indicating trade-offs in software quality. Usability results yielded a good rating (SUS = 71.4), while cognitive workload remained moderate (NASA-TLX = 55.5), reflecting reduced syntactic effort but increased linguistic reasoning. Thematic analysis identified trust calibration, loss of control, cognitive adaptation, and prompt-engineering strategy as key constructs. Notably, perceived loss of control was associated with increased security risks due to reduced transparency and validation of AI-generated outputs. Based on these findings, the study proposes a three-pillar framework for responsible adoption: hybrid integration of human and AI capabilities, human oversight and transparent accountability, and context-aware deployment. Overall, vibe coding enhances productivity but requires critical oversight, reinforcing its role as a transformative yet transitional paradigm in software development.

</details>


### [12] [XAgent: eXecution-guided Agentic AI for Effective Localization and Resolution of GitHub Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09769)
*Hieu Huynh,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Michael Fu,Bach Le,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出XAgent，一个执行引导的智能体框架，通过分析动态行为与额外程序上下文来定位和验证仓库级GitHub issue，在SWE-bench-lite上达到62.0%解决率和72.8%函数定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体方法依赖有限且静态的issue描述，导致定位错误与验证不完整；仅依靠描述会使LLM推理偏向狭隘范围，产生无法解决根本问题的残缺补丁。

Method: XAgent是一个执行引导（execution-guided）的智能体框架，通过分析动态执行行为和额外的程序上下文来定位并验证issue。

Result: 在SWE-bench-lite上，XAgent优于现有方法，解决率达62.0%，函数定位准确率72.8%，并保持成本效率；额外成功解决7个顶级基线无法处理的issue。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从静态、面向描述的补丁生成转向动态执行引导的issue解决，为基于LLM的编码智能体实现更稳健、更通用的软件维护开辟了新机会。

Abstract: Agentic AI has enabled capabilities in leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to autonomously resolve repository-level GitHub issues. However, due to the reliance on limited static description of issues, existing agentic approaches suffer from incorrect localization and incomplete validation. Solely relying on this information can bias LLM reasoning toward the narrow scope of the issue description, leading to incomplete patches that fail to address the underlying issue. In this paper, we present XAgent, an execution-guided agentic framework that analyzes dynamic behavior and additional program context to localize and validate issues. The experimental results on the SWE-bench-lite dataset demonstrate that XAgent outperforms other existing approaches, achieving a resolve rate of 62.0% and a function localization accuracy of 72.8%, while maintaining cost efficiency. Our analysis further shows that XAgent successfully resolves 7 additional issues that the top existing baselines fail to address. This work highlights a shift from static, description-oriented patch generation toward dynamic execution-guided issue resolution, opening new opportunities for LLM-based coding agents to achieve more robust and generalizable software maintenance.

</details>


### [13] [Keep Evaluation Fair: Detecting Data Leakage in Code Generation Benchmarks via Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09865)
*Dongdong Zhao,Jian Chen,Guancheng Lin,Jianwen Xiang,Jacky Wai Keung,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CGMIA，一种面向代码生成基准的成员推断攻击方法，通过结合代码相似度、功能正确性、语义表示与困惑度等多维特征，检测基准数据是否泄漏进LLM训练集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成基准数据泄漏会虚高模型性能、破坏评估有效性。现有方法DetectLeak仅依赖困惑度分数，困惑度主要反映对代码模式的一般熟悉度，在复杂或稀有样本上表现较差，且忽略了代码相似度、功能正确性和语义表示等有用信号。

Method: CGMIA首先在基准样本子集上微调影子模型，构造有标签的成员与非成员数据；对每个样本收集输入提示、生成代码和参考解，提取专家特征（CodeBLEU、编辑距离、测试通过率、困惑度）以及来自CodeBERT嵌入的语义特征；再通过集成学习模块融合这些特征，捕捉表层记忆信号与深层行为模式，训练分类器预测样本是否被纳入目标模型训练集。

Result: 在八个代码生成基准上，CGMIA在多数情况下优于八种现有成员推断方法，并能有效检测出StarCoder-7B训练数据中已知泄漏的APPS样本。

Conclusion: CGMIA通过融合专家特征与语义特征的集成学习，为代码生成基准的泄漏检测提供了更有效的方案，弥补了单纯依赖困惑度方法的不足。

Abstract: Code generation benchmarks are widely used to evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs), but benchmark data leakage into training sets can inflate performance and undermine evaluation validity. DetectLeak, a method specifically designed for code generation benchmark leakage detection, relies on perplexity scores to identify likely leaked samples. However, perplexity mainly reflects general familiarity with code patterns and may perform poorly on complex or rare samples. It also overlooks other useful signals, such as code similarity, functional correctness, and semantic representations. To address these limitations, we propose CGMIA (Code-Generation-specific Membership Inference Attack), a method for detecting leakage in code generation benchmarks. CGMIA fine-tunes a shadow model on a subset of benchmark samples to construct labeled member and non-member data. For each sample, it collects the input prompt, generated code, and reference solution, and extracts expert features, including CodeBLEU, edit distance, test pass rate, and perplexity, together with semantic features from CodeBERT embeddings. An integrated learning module combines these features to capture both surface-level memorization signals and deeper behavioral patterns, enabling a classifier to predict whether a sample was included in the target model's training set. Experiments on eight code generation benchmarks show that CGMIA outperforms eight existing membership inference methods in most cases. It also effectively detects known leaked APPS samples in StarCoder-7B's training data.

</details>


### [14] [Socio-technical and Ethical Dimensions of Architecture Practices in FLOSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09975)
*Sven Thielen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨FLOSS中软件架构实践如何受社会技术与伦理因素影响，并提出三阶段研究设计以产出实证证据、概念框架及轻量教学实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLOSS在数字主权中作用重要，但其架构决策常未被记录、散落于issue、PR和邮件列表中；既有研究关注架构制品、侵蚀与沟通，却未充分探讨FLOSS中架构工作、治理安排与伦理承诺之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用三阶段设计：(1)对3-4对架构上非平凡的FLOSS项目进行多方法案例研究；(2)与实践者和教育者共同开展框架与干预设计；(3)在项目与课程中开展试点评估。

Result: 预期产出：(i)关于FLOSS架构实践的跨案例实证证据；(ii)将架构实践与社会技术条件和伦理维度相联结的概念框架；(iii)使架构工作更显性、更具包容性的轻量实践与教学形式。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在支持更加显性、包容和反思性的架构工作，推动FLOSS架构实践与教育的发展。

Abstract: This project investigates how software architecture practices in Free/Libre Open Source Software (FLOSS) are shaped by socio-technical and ethical factors, and how education can support more explicit, inclusive, and reflective architectural work. Motivated by FLOSS's role in digital sovereignty, it is observed that architectural decisions are often undocumented and scattered across issues, pull requests, and mailing lists. While prior research has studied architectural artifacts, erosion, and communication, the interplay between architectural work, governance arrangements, and ethical commitments in FLOSS remains underexplored. The research follows a three-phase design: (1) multi-method case studies of 3-4 domain-pairs of architecturally non-trivial FLOSS projects, (2) framework and intervention design with practitioners and educators, and (3) pilot evaluations in projects and courses. It will produce (i) cross-case empirical evidence on FLOSS architecture practices, (ii) a conceptual framework linking architecture practices to socio-technical conditions and ethical dimensions, and (iii) lightweight practices and teaching formats that render architectural work more explicit and inclusive.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Repository Boundaries: Cross-Repository Graph Retrieval for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09987)
*Minh Le-Anh,Nam Le Hai,Quyen Tran,Anh Nguyen Hoang,Linh Ngo Van,Bach Le,Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出跨仓库代码生成框架 CrossCoder，通过统一知识图谱纳入外部库上下文，实现多跳证据检索，并引入 VersionExec 基准评估依赖版本兼容性，在功能正确性和版本鲁棒性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成不仅要求生成代码与目标仓库兼容，还需与其依赖环境兼容。现有基于检索的方法主要从本地仓库检索上下文，外部 API 使用依赖模型预训练知识，对未见或特定版本的 API 支持不足；同时检索策略多聚焦单跳证据，忽略代码组件间的结构关系。

Method: CrossCoder 是一个跨仓库代码生成框架，通过构建覆盖仓库与库实体的统一知识图谱，将外部库显式纳入检索上下文；先通过规划与语义检索识别重要节点，再有选择地扩展邻居节点，以获取更丰富的多跳上下文证据用于生成。此外提出 VersionExec，一个基于 BigCodeBench 派生、面向依赖版本兼容性的执行型基准。

Result: 在 RepoExec、DevEval 和 VersionExec 上的实验表明，CrossCoder 持续提升功能正确性（pass@1 最高提升 6.3%），并增强对依赖版本变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模外部库与多跳结构关系，CrossCoder 有效缓解了外部 API 知识与版本兼容性问题，并提供了评估依赖版本兼容性的新基准。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation requires generated code to be compatible not only with the target repository but also with its dependency environment. Existing retrieval-based methods mainly retrieve context from the local repository, leaving external API usage dependent on the model's pretrained knowledge, which can be insufficient for unseen or version-specific APIs. Moreover, current retrieval strategies largely focus on one-hop evidence and overlook the structural relationships among code components. We propose CrossCoder, a cross-repository code generation framework that explicitly incorporates external libraries into the retrieval context through a unified knowledge graph over repository and library entities. CrossCoder identifies important nodes via planning and semantic retrieval, then selectively expands neighboring nodes to retrieve richer multi-hop contextual evidence for generation. To further evaluate dependency-version compatibility, we introduce VersionExec, an execution-based benchmark derived from BigCodeBench that evaluates generation under different dependency versions. Experimental results on RepoExec, DevEval, and VersionExec demonstrate that CrossCoder consistently improves both functional correctness (up to 6.3% on pass@1) and robustness to dependency-version changes.

</details>


### [16] [GraphDroid: Asynchronous LLM-Based Mobile App GUI Testing via History-Aware Exploration and Hybrid Intent Fulfillment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10031)
*Xiaolei Li,Jialun Cao,Zhijian Hou,Yuzhi Zhao,Yepang Liu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GraphDroid是一个意图驱动的GUI测试框架，通过聚类记忆、异步意图生成和混合意图执行策略，提升移动应用测试覆盖率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化GUI测试难以覆盖需要多步操作序列的复杂功能。传统工具缺乏语义理解，难以合成此类序列；现有LLM工具存在三方面不足：1) 丢失历史上下文，难以识别未覆盖功能；2) 同步意图生成阻塞探索；3) 每步依赖LLM执行导致高成本和高延迟。

Method: 提出GraphDroid框架，包含：(1)基于聚类的记忆机制，从历史访问状态中识别未覆盖功能；(2)异步意图生成范式，消除延迟瓶颈；(3)混合意图执行策略，复杂意图交由LLM处理，简单意图委托给轻量启发式算法。

Result: 在41个真实Android应用上与6个先进基线对比，代码覆盖率最高提升36.4%，成本不到最佳纯LLM基线的八分之一；发现19个bug，并在Themis基准中检测出52个崩溃中的13个，均超过所有基线。19个bug中7个为未知bug并已上报，4个已被确认修复。

Conclusion: GraphDroid通过结合聚类记忆、异步生成与混合执行，有效提升GUI测试的覆盖率和效率，并具备较强的bug发现能力。

Abstract: Automated GUI testing is a widely adopted technique for ensuring mobile application quality by simulating user interactions to exercise functionalities. Despite the research breakthroughs in the past decades, covering complex functionalities that require multi-step action sequences still remains challenging. Traditional tools lack semantic understanding capability and can rarely synthesize such action sequences. Recent LLM-based tools can generate test intents describing target functionalities and leverage the LLM to fulfill the intents, but suffer from three key limitations: 1) loss of historical context for identifying uncovered functionalities, 2) synchronous intent generation that blocks exploration, and 3) per-step LLM-driven fulfillment incurring high cost and latency. To address these limitations, we propose GraphDroid, an intent-driven GUI testing framework that integrates a cluster-based memory mechanism to effectively identify uncovered functionalities from historically visited states for comprehensive application testing. For improving testing efficiency, GraphDroid adopts an asynchronous intent generation paradigm that eliminates the latency bottleneck and a hybrid intent fulfillment strategy that reserves the LLM for fulfilling complex intents while delegating simple intents to a lightweight heuristic algorithm. We evaluate GraphDroid on 41 real-world Android apps against six state-of-the-art baselines. Results show that GraphDroid outperforms all baselines, achieving up to 36.4% higher code coverage while incurring less than one eighth of the cost of the best pure LLM-based baseline. GraphDroid also exposes 19 bugs in the 41 apps and detects 13 of 52 crashes in the Themis bug benchmark, surpassing all the six baselines. Seven of the 19 bugs were previously unknown and we reported them to the developers. So far, four bugs have been confirmed and fixed.

</details>


### [17] [If It's Not Buggy, Don't Fix It: On the Dynamics of Iterative Bug-fixing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10123)
*Xietao Wang-Lin,Anton Isopoussu,Louis Mahon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该报告研究LLM作为自动修bug工具的迭代盲目使用，发现LLM会在完全无bug的程序中"检测"出bug，且对正确程序的破坏率高于对buggy程序的修复率，并常陷入反复增删同一改动的伪修复循环；机制探针揭示存在控制编辑倾向的steering vector。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动程序修复工具在代码审查中日益普及，但对其自主迭代使用的可靠性与长期动态缺乏了解，尤其担心其盲目修bug的行为，因此作者希望探究这一问题。

Method: 在多个模型和修复环境中进行迭代式的"盲目修bug"实验，观察修复率与破坏率；分析迭代过程的长期动态；并通过mechanistic probing找出与"buggy code"内部表征相关的steering vector。

Result: LLM一致声称在完全无bug的程序中检测到bug；对buggy程序的修复率低于对正确程序的破坏率；迭代过程常进入伪修复循环（同一改动反复加入又删除）；发现一个控制编辑倾向的steering vector，其被错误激活导致伪修复。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了全自主修bug系统的动态特性，并为目标模糊情况下的停止条件提供了启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous in software development, with LLM-based automated program repair tools increasingly used during code review. In this report, we explore the iterative blind use of LLMs as bug-fixers. Across multiple models and repair environments, we find that LLMs consistently claim to detect bugs in entirely bug-free programs while the rate of repair of buggy programs is less than that of the damage to correct programs. We also explore the long-term dynamics of this iterative process, and find that this frequently reaches a pseudo-bug-fixing cycle where the same changes are added and removed again ad infinitum. Lastly, via mechanistic probing, we unveil the existence of a steering vector which controls the editing propensity, suggesting that LLMs have an internal representation of ``buggy code", and that this representation is what is falsely activated to induce pseudo-bug fixing. These results provide insight towards the dynamics of fully autonomous bug-fixing systems, as well as stopping conditions under ambiguous goals.

</details>


### [18] [A-JIT: Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10248)
*Mark Marron,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体即时软件构建"(A-JIT)新范式，用可持续演化的动态软件系统取代静态二进制交付，通过在应用内嵌入AI智能体实时观测使用与执行轨迹，动态合成缺失实现并生成新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件交付基于静态范式：代码在执行前构建并作为固定制品部署，难以适应不断变化的需求和终端用户的具体使用情境。

Method: A-JIT将应用视为代码、运行时框架与嵌入式AI智能体的集成体。智能体持续观测系统使用情况与实时执行轨迹，类比传统JIT编译器将机器码特化到运行路径，A-JIT将软件逻辑、工作流与工具接口特化到终端用户需求；并将合成过程直接融入应用生命周期。

Result: 该模型支持应用动态构建缺失的实现、即时生成新能力，并持续适应用户行为；同时支持轨迹驱动的人机协同构建。

Conclusion: A-JIT为自适应、自演化软件开辟了新的设计空间，是软件构建与交付范式的一次转变。

Abstract: Traditional software delivery assumes a static paradigm: code is constructed prior to execution and deployed as a fixed artifact. We present Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction (A-JIT), a paradigm that replaces static binaries with dynamic, software systems that can perpetually evolve to meet changing demands. In A-JIT, an application is an integrated assembly comprising code, a runtime harness, and an embedded AI agent that continuously observes system usage and live execution traces. Much like a traditional JIT compiler specializes machine code to runtime execution paths, A-JIT specializes software logic, workflows, and tool interfaces to meet the specific needs of the end-user. By integrating synthesis directly into the ambient application lifecycle, A-JIT enables applications to dynamically construct missing implementations, generate new capabilities on the fly, and continuously adapt to end-user behavior. We demonstrate how this model supports trace-driven human-AI co-construction and opens a new design space for adaptive, self-evolving software.

</details>


### [19] [Ensembling LLMs for AI-Augmented Cybersecurity Software Requirements Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10316)
*Santiago Perez-Acuna,Yod-Samuel Martín,Juan C. Yelmo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究将LLM在安全需求翻译中的随机性视为资源，提出基于信息检索数据融合算子的生成后集成方法（Uniform与Naive-Bayes融合），在ISO/IEC 27002控制项上将多次运行的输出聚合，显著提升有效需求覆盖并抑制幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将安全标准中的高层控制转化为具体、系统特定的需求是网络安全需求工程的核心，但该任务劳动密集且对召回敏感。LLM可加速该任务，但单次运行不可靠：既会遗漏有效防护措施，又会引入看似合理的幻觉，且不同运行与模型之间输出不稳定。作者不再只选择单一输出，而是将这种变异性重新界定为可利用的资源。

Method: 研究生成后集成（post-generation ensembling），用信息检索领域的数据融合算子聚合多次随机运行。提出两种策略：Uniform融合（奖励跨运行的单纯一致性）与Naive-Bayes融合（根据每次运行的估计可靠性进行加权）。评估基于12种配置、4个模型族共24次运行，针对10个ISO/IEC 27002:2022控制项生成结果，并由专家对照72条有效需求的黄金标准进行评判。

Result: 汇集所有运行输出可回收全部72条有效需求（而单一配置平均仅回收不到一半），但同时引入111条幻觉。融合方法能将有效需求排在幻觉之前。在PR-AUC和ROC-AUC上，仅Uniform融合即比最佳单一配置分别高出0.142和0.118；Naive-Bayes加权再增加0.039和0.052，达到0.864和0.869，并更早获得可用工作点。内部验证显示增益稳定：在至少92%的袋外自助重采样及所有结构化扰动样本中保持正向。

Conclusion: 生成后融合将表面噪声转化为实用资产：一个轻量层即可为分析人员提供更广的覆盖范围和更优优先级的审查队列，且仅使用经济、非前沿的模型即可实现。

Abstract: Translating high-level controls from security standards into concrete, system-specific requirements is central to cybersecurity requirements engineering. Large language models (LLMs) can accelerate this labor-intensive, recall-sensitive task, but any single run is unreliable: it misses valid safeguards while introducing plausible hallucinations, and outputs shift across runs and models. We reframe this variability as a resource: rather than selecting one output, we study post-generation ensembling, aggregating stochastic runs with information-retrieval data-fusion operators. We propose two strategies: Uniform fusion rewards mere cross-run agreement, whereas Naive-Bayes fusion weights each run by its estimated reliability. We evaluate both over 24 runs from 12 configurations across four model families, generated for ten ISO/IEC 27002:2022 controls and expert-judged against a gold standard of 72 valid requirements. Pooling every run's output recovers all 72 (whereas single configurations recover on average under half) but also 111 hallucinations. Fusion separates the wheat from the chaff, ranking valid requirements well ahead of hallucinations. In the areas under the precision-recall and ROC curves, Uniform fusion alone largely surpasses every original run and configuration by 0.142 and 0.118 over the best configuration. Naive-Bayes weighting adds a further 0.039 and 0.052, reaching 0.864 and 0.869 while attaining useful operating points earlier. Internal validation confirms the stability of these gains: they stay positive in at least 92% of out-of-bag bootstrap resamples and every structured-perturbation sample. Post-generation fusion thus turns apparent noise into a practical asset: a lightweight layer giving analysts broader coverage and a better prioritized review queue, using affordable, below-frontier models alone.

</details>


### [20] [Retrofitting Code Using LLMs to Support Exceptional Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10397)
*Linghan Zhong,Jiyang Zhang,Jayanth Srinivasa,Junyi Jessy Li,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"为代码补充异常相关代码(ERC)"的新任务,并设计 EXCODER,结合静态/动态程序分析与大模型(LLM),根据异常行为测试(EBT)自动生成缺失的 throw 语句、守卫条件与 try/catch 块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常相关代码(ERC)是软件系统的重要组成,可帮助开发者检测与处理偏离预期行为的异常状态。但在大型代码库中手工编写 ERC 十分繁琐,因此需要自动化手段来为现有代码"补装"ERC。

Method: 提出新任务:给定不含 ERC 的代码与异常行为测试(EBT),自动生成缺失的 ERC 使测试通过。实现 EXCODER:通过"上下文工程"结合静态与动态程序分析,提取上下文信息提供给 LLM,辅助其完成生成。

Result: 基于 GitHub Java 仓库构建基准,在 75 个项目的 304 个方法中系统移除 ERC。结合 Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b,在开发者编写的测试套件上 pass@1/5/10 分别达 85.92%/86.18%/86.51%,较基线提升 12.56/12.82/13.15 个百分点。

Conclusion: EXCODER 为该问题提供了有效但非完美的自动化代码生成方案,首次让开发者能按测试驱动开发方式实现 ERC;人工检查也揭示了其局限,指明未来工作方向。

Abstract: Exception Related Code (ERC), which includes throw statements, conditions (if statements) that guard those throw statements, and try/catch blocks, is an essential component of software systems, allowing developers to detect and handle exceptional states that deviate from the expected program behavior. However, manually writing ERC across large codebases is tedious. We propose a novel task: retrofitting existing code with ERC. Namely, given code (without ERC) and Exceptional Behavior Tests (EBTs) (e.g., check if method throws InvalidArgumentException if null is given as the value to the argument) we aim to automatically generate missing ERC, such that the given tests pass. We design and implement Exception Coder (EXCODER) that performs context engineering to help Large Language Models (LLMs) tackle this task. EXCODER integrates static and dynamic program analysis with LLMs by providing the extracted contextual information to the LLMs. To evaluate EXCODER, we build a benchmark constructed from GitHub Java repositories, where we systematically remove ERC in 304 methods from 75 projects. Our results demonstrate that EXCODER provides an effective, though imperfect, solution to this problem in automated code generation, offering developers the first way to implement ERC following test-driven development. When combined with Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b, EXCODER achieves pass@1, 5, and 10 rates of 85.92% (12.56 percentage points over baseline), 86.18% (12.82 p.p. over baseline), and 86.51% (13.15 p.p. over baseline), respectively, on developer-written test suites. Our manual inspection of the generated code further reveals limitations of EXCODER, pointing to directions for future work.

</details>


### [21] [Towards Scalable and Cost-Efficient Vulnerability Detection: A Study on Automatic Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10412)
*Ivana Clairine Irsan,Ratnadira Widyasari,Huihui Huang,Ting Zhang,Yue Liu,Ouh Eng Lieh,Shar Lwin Khin,Kang Hong Jin,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究LLM基于NVD漏洞数据合成CodeQL查询的能力，结果表明LLM生成的查询显著提升基线检测效果，平均F1-score提高82%，且相比直接扫描全仓库更具成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析（如CodeQL）是软件安全的重要基石，但开发高覆盖率的查询套件需要大量人工投入。LLM虽被视为自动代码推理的潜在方案，但其生成结构化、可执行安全查询的实用价值尚未被充分探索。

Method: 利用国家漏洞数据库（NVD）中的漏洞数据，开展实证研究评估LLM合成CodeQL查询的能力；系统地评测多种LLM架构在多样化真实漏洞上的表现，衡量其对检测覆盖率和精确率的提升。

Result: LLM生成的查询显著优于基线CodeQL查询，平均F1-score提升82%。成本效益分析显示，直接用LLM扫描整个代码仓库在计算和经济上往往不可行，而借助LLM合成CodeQL查询则是大规模漏洞检测的可扩展、高性价比替代方案。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效弥合非结构化漏洞报告与形式化静态分析规范之间的鸿沟，为全面自动化漏洞检测提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Static analysis remains a cornerstone of software security, yet the effectiveness of tools such as CodeQL is often limited by the substantial manual effort required to develop high-coverage query suites. While large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a potential solution for automated code reasoning, their practical utility in generating structured, executable security queries remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study to evaluate the ability of LLMs to synthesize CodeQL queries using vulnerability data from the National Vulnerability Database. Through this investigation, we explore the potential of using LLMs as an automatic CodeQL query generator. Subsequently, we systematically evaluate the performance of various LLM architectures across a diverse set of real-world vulnerabilities, measuring their ability to improve detection coverage and precision. Our findings reveal that LLM-generated queries significantly enhance the baseline CodeQL queries, yielding 82% improvement in average F1-score. Furthermore, we provide a detailed cost- benefit analysis showing that while direct LLM-based scanning of entire repositories is often computationally and financially prohibitive, leveraging LLMs to synthesize CodeQL queries offers a scalable and cost-effective alternative for large-scale vulnerability detection. Our results suggest that LLMs can effectively bridge the gap between unstructured vulnerability reports and formal static analysis specifications, offering a scalable path toward comprehensive automated vulnerability detection.

</details>

### [22] [MotionCanvas: Learning Implicit Motion Planning from Composable Kinematic Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10457)
*Zeyu Ling,Di Kang,Qing Shuai,Yuxin Wen,Jing Li,Zhanke Wang,Heng Li,Changqing Zou,Chunchao Guo,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出 MotionCanvas，一种支持"线索条件隐式运动规划"的模型，可在共享运动画布上统一异质运动学线索，生成全局连贯的全身动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业角色动画既需要自然的动作，也需要精确、多样的控制。创作者常常需要指定动作时机、手臂摆动幅度、行走路径等运动学线索，而现有方法难以将这些稀疏或稠密、完整或部分的异质线索忠实且连贯地整合为一个全身动作序列。

Method: MotionCanvas 在共享的"运动画布"上表示异质运动学线索，即跨身体关节与时间定义位置和旋转值。使用共享的流匹配（flow-matching）模型以该画布为条件生成动作，并可选用语言和输入动作作为附加条件；通过线索插补（cue imputation）在训练和采样中保持指定画布值固定。为学习跨不同线索集合的连贯补全，采用组合式线索采样器，随机变化线索施加时机、指定哪些位置或旋转、以及它们的组合方式。

Result: 在时间、根节点和身体部位线索（单独及组合）及语言引导编辑上测试规划能力，并扩展到序列生成与动作修复任务。MotionCanvas 在受控动作质量、混合线索遵循度、序列生成、指令编辑和动作修复上均取得最优结果，同时保持文本到动作的能力。

Conclusion: 单一生成器即可综合出全局连贯、同时满足兼容异质线索的动作；组合式线索采样与线索插补设计使模型具备统一的运动规划与补全能力，可自然泛化到多种下游控制任务。

Abstract: Professional character animation requires both natural motion and precise, versatile control. For example, it is common for the creators to define the timing of a specified action, to control the motion range of the character's arm swing, and the route the character walks through, like specifying various kinematic motion cues on a ``motion canvas''. This motivates us to propose MotionCanvas, a model that supports \emph{cue-conditioned implicit motion planning} to faithfully and coherently connect all cues, dense or sparse, full or partial, into one full-body motion sequence. Specifically, MotionCanvas represents heterogeneous kinematic cues on a shared motion canvas, where position and rotation values are specified across body joints and time. A shared flow-matching model generates motion conditioned on this canvas, with optional language and input motion; cue imputation keeps the specified canvas values fixed in both training and sampling. To learn coherent completion across different cue sets, we train with a compositional cue sampler that varies when cues are applied, which positions or rotations are specified, and how they are combined.
  Together, these designs enable a single generator to synthesize globally coherent actions that jointly satisfy compatible heterogeneous cues. We test this planning ability with temporal, root, and body-part cues---alone and in combination---and language-guided editing. We naturally extend this evaluation to sequential generation and motion repair, since both require the same ability to organize coherent motion from kinematic cues. Across these evaluations, MotionCanvas establishes state-of-the-art results in controlled-motion quality, mixed-cue adherence, sequential generation, instruction editing, and motion repair while preserving its text-to-motion capability.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [23] [LBFAST: A Lightweight Moment-Represented Lattice Boltzmann Solver for Multi-GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09160)
*Marco Lauricella,Andrea Montessori,Giorgio Amati,Filippo Spiga,Adriano Tiribocchi,Massimo Bernaschi,Sauro Succi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LBFAST，一种基于轻量级矩表示形式的GPU导向格子玻尔兹曼求解器，通过从约简矩集合动态重建碰撞后分布函数而非显式存储，大幅降低显存占用，并在多GPU系统上实现近理想的弱扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代加速器架构中显存容量和带宽是关键资源，传统格子玻尔兹曼方法需显式存储碰撞后分布函数，内存占用大，限制了大规模三维模拟的开展。

Method: 采用轻量级矩表示形式，在GPU上即时（on the fly）从约简的矩集合重建碰撞后分布函数，而非显式存储，从而降低内存占用。

Result: 通过标准单组分和双组分基准测试验证了良好的精度与稳定性；多GPU扩展实验显示在多达512块GPU上实现近理想弱扩展，并在不同速度集下保持持续性能。

Conclusion: 该形式结合了降低的内存占用、具有竞争力的吞吐量以及稳定的能效，为在当前及新兴HPC平台上开展大规模格子玻尔兹曼模拟提供了一条实用途径。

Abstract: We present LBFAST, a GPU-oriented lattice Boltzmann solver based on a lightweight moment-represented formulation, in which post-collision populations are reconstructed on the fly from a reduced set of moments rather than stored explicitly. This approach significantly lowers the memory footprint, enabling large three-dimensional simulations within the constraints of modern accelerator architectures, where VRAM capacity and bandwidth are critical resources. The method is assessed through standard single- and two-component benchmarks demonstrating good accuracy and stability. Extensive scaling experiments on multi-GPU systems show near-ideal weak scaling up to 512 GPUs and sustained performance across different velocity sets. The combination of reduced memory usage, competitive throughput, and stable energy efficiency makes the proposed formulation a practical route for large-scale lattice Boltzmann simulations on current and emerging HPC platforms.

</details>


### [24] [SMCC-Empowered Digital Twins for Sensorless Monitoring in Large-Scale AI-Driven IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09161)
*Vincenzo Sammartino*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SMCC-DT框架，利用6G边缘ISAC波形实现无传感器监测，并通过PPO深度强化学习进行跨层资源优化，显著降低数字孪生同步时延与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模物联网中AI驱动的数字孪生需要物理环境与虚拟副本之间持续、高保真的同步，而传统方案依赖密集传感器部署，在硬件、能量和网络带宽方面成本过高。论文旨在以通感一体化（ISAC）替代密集传感，降低部署成本并保障同步性能。

Method: 提出集成感知-记忆-通信-计算（SMCC）的SMCC-DT框架：单一无线电信号同时完成环境遥测提取（感知）与边缘服务器传输（通信），边缘侧将大规模AI模型加载至受限内存（记忆）并执行（计算）以更新数字孪生状态。将孪生同步问题建模为跨层优化，联合分配发射功率、波束成形向量、内存分区和CPU频率，在感知精度、吞吐量、内存容量和计算预算约束下最小化端到端同步时延。由于该混合整数非线性规划为NP-hard，设计基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习智能体SMCCAGENT在线学习近优资源分配策略。

Result: 在500节点工业物联网测试平台上的大量仿真表明：相比正交方案与仅计算基线，SMCC-DT将数字孪生同步时延降低38.7%，总能耗降低27.4%，同时保持感知精度高于95%，模型推理吞吐量高于30帧/秒。

Conclusion: SMCC-DT通过融合ISAC、边缘计算与强化学习资源调度，为大规模物联网数字孪生提供了一种低时延、低能耗且高精度的无传感器同步方案，验证了跨层联合优化与DRL在线决策的有效性。

Abstract: The deployment of AI-driven Digital Twins (DTs) in large-scale Internet-of-Things (IoT) ecosystems demands continuous, high-fidelity synchronization between the physical environment and its virtual replica. Conventional approaches rely on dense sensor deployments, which introduce prohibitive costs in terms of hardware, energy, and network bandwidth. In this paper, we propose SMCC-DT, an integrated Sensing-Memory-Communication-Computation (SMCC) framework that enables sensorless monitoring of physical assets by exploiting Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) waveforms at the 6G Edge. Under the SMCC-DT paradigm, a single radio signal simultaneously extracts environmental telemetry (Sensing) and delivers it to an Edge server (Communication), where a large-scale AI model is loaded into constrained memory (Memory) and executed (Computation) to update the DT state. We formulate the DT synchronization problem as a cross-layer optimization that jointly allocates transmit power, beamforming vectors, memory partitions, and CPU frequency to minimize the end-to-end synchronization latency subject to sensing accuracy, throughput, memory capacity, and computational budget constraints. Because the resulting mixed-integer nonlinear program is NP-hard, we design a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agent, termed SMCCAGENT, that learns near-optimal resource allocation policies online. Extensive simulations over a 500-node industrial IoT testbed demonstrate that SMCC-DT reduces DT synchronization latency by 38.7% and total energy consumption by 27.4% compared to state-of-the-art orthogonal and compute-only baselines, while sustaining sensing accuracy above 95% and model inference throughput above 30 frames per second.

</details>


### [25] [HELIOS: Guardrailed LLM-Driven Evolution of Autonomous Resource Orchestration Policies for Multi-Cloud Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09164)
*Guanyu Ding,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HELIOS 将 LLM 移出关键路径，让 LLM 离线演化可执行编排策略，仅将冠军程序部署到生产并加装安全护栏，在真实负载与多云价格数据下显著降低成本并保持高可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多云环境下的延迟敏感服务面临 vCPU 价格差异、竞价折扣与中断风险不同、出口费用惩罚状态迁移、单云故障等问题，这是单一云自动扩缩器无法看到的优化空间。LLM 虽能从自然语言描述合成决策逻辑，但若把 LLM 放入每次调度决策的关键路径，对 200 服务规模而言推理成本会超过所管理的云容量，并给毫秒级控制回路增加数秒延迟。

Method: HELIOS 把 LLM 移出关键路径：LLM 在基于真实 trace 的多云模拟器上，针对固定特征接口演化出小型 Python 可执行编排策略程序；生产中只运行冠军程序，并由护栏强制容量可行性、SLO 分级放置规则与变更预算，无论演化代码提出什么建议。

Result: 在 PlanetLab、Bitbrains、Azure 真实负载 trace 结合 AWS、Azure、GCP 的 2026 价格与中断频率数据上，演化策略相较单云 best-fit 基线降低受罚运营成本 45%，相较校准的多云启发式降低 9-19%；以低 40% 成本达到 oracle 知情 MILP 规划器的高端 SLO 性能，并优于用 2.6 倍环境交互训练的 DQN 元控制器。护栏至关重要：无护栏时同一策略族在 10 倍竞价风险压力测试下退化到 97-98% 高端层停机，而有护栏时为 0.17%。

Conclusion: LLM 适合作为离线策略演化器而非在线决策器，配合强制护栏可在多云延迟敏感服务编排中同时实现显著降本与高可用，并释放模拟器、策略、LLM 提示/响应档案与逐代适应度日志以保证可复现。

Abstract: Operating latency-sensitive services across multiple public clouds creates an optimization surface no single provider autoscaler can see: on-demand vCPU prices differ by provider, spot discounts and interruption risks vary by provider and instance type, egress fees penalize state movement, and provider-level failures can take down single-cloud deployments. Large language models (LLMs) are appealing orchestrators because they can synthesize non-trivial decision logic from a natural-language environment description. However, placing an LLM on the critical path of every scheduling decision is impractical: for a 200-service fleet, per-decision inference would cost more than the cloud capacity it manages and add multi-second latency to a millisecond-scale control loop. HELIOS moves the LLM off the critical path. The LLM evolves executable orchestration policies, small Python programs over a fixed feature interface, against a trace-driven multi-cloud simulator. Only the champion program runs in production, wrapped in guardrails that enforce capacity feasibility, SLO-class placement rules, and churn budgets regardless of evolved code's proposal. On real workload traces (PlanetLab, Bitbrains, Azure) combined with 2026 price and interruption-frequency data from AWS, Azure, and GCP, the evolved policy reduces penalized operating cost by 45% versus a single-cloud best-fit baseline and by 9-19% versus calibrated multi-cloud heuristics. It matches an oracle-informed MILP planner's premium-SLO performance at 40% lower cost and outperforms a DQN meta-controller trained with 2.6x more environment interactions. Guardrails are essential: without them, the same policy family degrades to 97-98% premium-tier downtime under a 10x spot-hazard stress test, versus 0.17% when guarded. We release the simulator, policies, LLM prompt/response archive, and per-generation fitness logs for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [26] [TFR-GNN: Topology- and Fault-Aware Graph Neural Scheduling for Heterogeneous Distributed Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09165)
*Shiyu Yang,Ziyang Zeng,Jie-Si Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出TFR-GNN图神经调度器，通过容错oracle蒸馏实现一次决策，在无故障时与HEFT一致，有故障时平均降低期望完成时间14.8%，并可泛化到更大规模工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构图上的工作流DAG调度是NP难问题，HEFT因低复杂度和强makespan成为事实标准。但真实部署中机器会故障，忽视可靠性的最优makespan布局会因节点故障而严重变慢，且实验表明不存在在故障强度与集群负载联合空间中普遍最优的固定启发式。

Method: TFR-GNN结合任务DAG上的双向依赖注意力、带宽加权机器图上的拓扑注意力，以及带故障门控可靠性倾斜和可选复制门的交叉注意力放置头；通过将最优组合容错oracle蒸馏为单一一次性策略进行训练。

Result: 在真实工作流和双峰可靠性集群模型上，无故障时TFR-GNN与HEFT完全一致；有故障时相比HEFT平均降低期望makespan 14.8%（最高47%），比固定可靠性基线R-HEFT优11%，作为单一策略匹配逐场景事后oracle且无需部署时调参；可泛化到未见应用和大一个数量级的工作流，近5000任务图调度时间低于1秒。

Conclusion: TFR-GNN通过故障感知的图神经调度与oracle蒸馏，在保持HEFT无故障优势的同时显著提升故障场景下的鲁棒性，具备良好泛化性和低推理延迟，所有结果均由真实工作流数据上的事件级模拟器验证。

Abstract: Scheduling workflow directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) on heterogeneous distributed systems is a classical NP-hard problem, and the list-scheduling heuristic HEFT remains the defacto standard because of its low complexity and strong makespan. In real deployments, however, machines fail: commodity and pre-emptible nodes are far less reliable than dedicated ones, and a makespan-optimal but reliability-agnostic placement can be dramatically slowed by node failures. We show empirically, on real workflow structures from the WfCommons/Pegasus corpus, that no single fixed heuristic is best across the joint space of failure intensity and cluster load: with no failures HEFT is optimal, whereas under failures a reliability-aware placement can reduce the expected makespan by up to 52% when spare capacity exists. Motivated by this, we present TFR-GNN, a graph neural scheduler that combines bidirectional dependency attention over the task DAG, topology attention over the (bandwidth-weighted) machine graph, and a cross-attention placement head augmented with a failure-gated reliability tilt and an optional replication gate. We train TFR-GNN by distilling a best-of-portfolio fault-tolerant oracle into a single one-shot policy. On real workflows and a bimodal-reliability cluster model, TFR-GNN matches HEFT exactly when there are no failures, reduces the expected makespan under failures by 14.8% on average (up to 47%) over HEFT, beats a fixed reliability-aware baseline (R-HEFT) by 11% and matches a per-scenario hindsight oracle as a single policy without any deployment-time tuning, and generalises to unseen applications and to workflows an order of magnitude larger than those seen in training, while producing schedules in well under a second for graphs of nearly 5,000 tasks. All results are produced by a verified event-level simulator on real workflow data; no experimental numbers are synthetic.

</details>


### [27] [Smart Adaptive Computing Across the Continuum: LLMs in IoT-Edge-Cloud Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09348)
*Antonino Vaccarella,Lanpei Li,Vincenzo Lomonaco,Massimo Coppola*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在Wang等人的DRL持续编排系统分类法基础上扩展两个维度（AI增强范式、反馈通道），对六个近期系统架构进行分类分析，发现无一系统在云持续环境中同时实现完整LLM编排与完整代理层反馈，并将此空白归因于缺失的跨层反馈抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT、边缘与云多层资源管理需要在不断变化的约束下进行持续的上下文感知决策，DRL适合此类问题，LLM也日益用于增强DRL流程，但二者之间的架构关系很少被明确阐述。本文旨在厘清LLM与DRL在持续编排中的架构关系，并识别现有系统的结构性缺口。

Method: 以Wang等人关于采用DRL技术的持续编排系统分类法为基础，扩展两个维度：AI增强范式（衡量LLM被如何利用）和反馈通道（捕捉执行反馈是否以及经由哪条系统路径返回LLM，从而在LLM编排层闭合MAPE控制环）。将扩展后的分类法应用于六个近期系统架构。

Result: 六个近期系统架构均存在共同缺口：没有一个在云持续环境中同时结合完整的LLM编排与完整的代理层反馈。

Conclusion: 该缺口与一个缺失的跨层反馈抽象有关，这一抽象本应桥接不可通约的各层信号与LLM编排器。研究由此指出未来系统设计的关键方向。

Abstract: Managing resources across IoT, edge, and cloud layers calls for continuous, context-aware decisions under constraints that rarely stay fixed. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) handles this class of problems well, and large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to augment DRL pipelines, yet the architectural relationship between the two is seldom made explicit. We build on Wang et al.'s taxonomy of Continuum Orchestration Systems employing DRL techniques and extend it with two further dimensions. The AI Augmentation Paradigm measures how LLMs are exploited, while the Feedback channel captures whether and through which system path the execution feedback returns to the LLM in order to close the MAPE control loop at the LLM Orchestration layer. We apply this taxonomy to six recent system architectures and find a common gap, as none combines full LLM orchestration with full agent-layer feedback in a Cloud Continuum setting. We relate this gap to a missing cross-tier feedback abstraction, bridging the incommensurable per-tier signals and the LLM Orchestrator.

</details>


### [28] [Breaking Fault Lines: Unifying TEE-Assisted BFT Consensus in Partially Trusted Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09742)
*Xiaoqing Wen,Tong Liu,Jianyu Niu,Jialin Li,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究通用部分TEE模型下的BFT共识，推导出紧致容错上界 f < max{n/3, m/2}，并提出Raftel与chained-Raftel协议，在性能上显著优于HotStuff。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE辅助BFT多假设所有或固定数量副本运行于TEE中，缺乏对任意部分TEE部署（部分副本有硬件信任、其余无）的系统性理解。本文旨在刻画异构信任对法定人数构成与容错能力的影响。

Method: 在通用部分TEE模型下分析容错结构，推导紧致弹性上界，并提出两条协议原则：双法定人数构造（安全结合纯TEE与混合法定人数）与TEE领导者快路径（利用硬件强制的不可抵赖性降低共识与视图切换延迟）。基于此实现HotStuff风格的Raftel及流水线变体chained-Raftel，并在Intel SGX上实现，于LAN与WAN环境评测。

Result: Raftel在WAN环境下达到最高625 TPS、延迟低于670 ms；吞吐量较HotStuff最高提升308 TPS，同时接近全TEE辅助协议的性能。

Conclusion: TEEs仅在超过部署三分之二时才提升容错能力，呈现明显阈值现象；Raftel是首个面向任意部分TEE部署的HotStuff风格BFT协议，兼顾安全性与性能。

Abstract: This paper revisits TEE-assisted BFT under a universal partial-TEE model, where an arbitrary subset of replicas execute inside TEEs while the remaining replicas operate without hardware trust guarantees. We show that heterogeneous trust changes the structure of quorum formation and fault tolerance. In particular, we derive a tight resilience bound f < max {n/3, m/2}, where n is the total number of replicas and m is the number of TEE-enabled replicas. The result reveals a sharp threshold phenomenon: TEEs improve fault tolerance only once they exceed two-thirds of the deployment.
  Guided by this characterization, we introduce two protocol principles: (1) a dual-quorum construction that safely combines TEE-only and mixed quorums, and (2) a TEE-leader fast path that leverages hardware-enforced non-equivocation to reduce both consensus and view-change latency. We realize these ideas in Raftel, which is, to our knowledge, the first HotStuff-style BFT protocol designed explicitly for arbitrary partial-TEE deployments, and in chained-Raftel, a pipelined variant that further accelerates mixed-trust execution. We implement both protocols atop Intel SGX and evaluate them in LAN and WAN environments. Our results show that Raftel achieves up to 625 TPS with sub-670 ms latency in WAN settings, outperforming HotStuff by up to 308 TPS in throughput while approaching the performance of fully TEE-assisted protocols.

</details>


### [29] [Epoch: Compiling Diffusion Blocks for Sparse MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09748)
*Jianian Zhu,Hang Wu,Yinghui Li,Haojie Wang,Ruixuan Li,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 \sys{}，一个将扩散块视为编译单元的 MoE 服务系统，通过 \atlas{}、\lsp{}、\freshlane{} 三个机制优化稠密 MoE 运行时的冗余计算与通信，在 8 张 H100 上实现最高 2.7 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型通过多次前向传播精炼固定大小的 token 块，与主流 LLM 服务系统的单次前向执行单元不匹配。稠密 MoE 运行时将全部工作绑定在精炼迭代时钟上，导致每次前向重建相似路由结构、对已失效 logits 位置重复计算专家输出，并通过稠密专家并行集合通信传输这些位置，造成大量浪费。

Method: \sys{} 将一个扩散块作为编译单元，编译出面向块时钟结构的"块计划"，并在迭代时钟上刷新所有可能影响活跃解码决策的值。具体沿 MoE 前向的三个稠密轴实现：\atlas{} 按层编译覆盖驱动的活跃专家支持集，同时每次迭代重算门控 logits；\lsp{} 保留完整序列分片作为模型状态，但仅将活跃、新解码和需刷新的位置路由到新的路由专家计算；\freshlane{} 将新鲜 token-expert 工作列表贯穿专家并行分发、核函数与合并，并在层边界恢复稠密逻辑分片。

Result: 在 8 张 NVIDIA H100 GPU 上实现 \sys{}，并在三个开放权重块扩散 MoE 模型（LLaDA-MoE、LLaDA2.0-mini、LLaDA2.0-Flash，总参数 7B 至 100B）和 GSM8K、HumanEval、MGSM、MT-Bench 四个基准上评估。相比最强保留基线，在相同 8-GPU 部署下端到端执行时间最高提升 2.7 倍，在多个基线内存不足的最大批次下仍可行，同时相对稠密参考保持任务质量。

Conclusion: 将扩散块作为编译单元的 \sys{} 系统通过活跃专家支持、活跃位置路由与新鲜工作列表通道，有效消除稠密 MoE 运行时在扩散语言模型服务中的路由重建、冗余专家计算与稠密集合通信开销，实现显著加速且不损失任务质量，并支持更大批次。

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate text by refining a fixed-size block of token positions through many forward passes, a loop that does not match the per-forward execution unit used by most LLM serving systems. A dense MoE runtime binds all work to the refinement-iteration clock: it rebuilds similar routing structure on every forward, recomputes expert outputs for positions whose logits are already dead, and sends those positions through dense expert-parallel collectives. This paper presents \sys{}, a serving system that treats the diffusion block as a compilation unit. \sys{} compiles a small \emph{block plan} for the block-clock structure of one diffusion block and refreshes every value that can affect a live decode decision on the iteration clock.
  \sys{} realizes this plan along three dense axes of an MoE forward: \atlas{} compiles a coverage-driven active expert support per layer while recomputing gate logits every iteration; \lsp{} keeps full sequence shards as model state but routes only live, newly decoded, and refresh-required positions through fresh routed-expert computation; \freshlane{} carries this fresh token--expert worklist through expert-parallel dispatch, kernels, and combine, then restores the dense logical shard at the layer boundary. We implement \sys{} on 8 NVIDIA H100 GPUs and evaluate it on three open-weight block-diffusion MoE models (LLaDA-MoE, LLaDA2.0-mini, and LLaDA2.0-Flash, spanning 7B to 100B total parameters) across GSM8K, HumanEval, MGSM, and MT-Bench. \sys{} improves end-to-end execution time by up to 2.7$\times$ over the strongest surviving baseline under the same 8-GPU placement and remains feasible at the largest batch sizes where multiple baselines run out of memory, while preserving task quality relative to the dense reference.

</details>


### [30] [Introvert Clustering for Distributed Graph Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10044)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Nima Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的图分解原语"内向聚类"(introvert clustering)，强化了标准低直径聚类，保证每个被聚类的顶点至少保留其相关邻居的 (1/2-ε) 在自己簇内。反复应用可得到 O(log n) 层、弱直径 O(log n) 的层状内向网络分解，并应用于 LOCAL 模型下的边染色与局部平衡割问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准网络分解的贪心范式在求解某些全局约束问题（如 (3/2+ε)Δ-边染色、局部平衡割）时受限，需要一个更强的分解保证，使每个顶点在局部保持足够比例的邻居关系，从而支持更广泛的处理流程。

Method: 提出内向聚类原语：每个聚类顶点保留至少 (1/2-ε) 的相关邻居在同一簇内；反复应用构造层状内向网络分解。随机版本使用 Miller–Peng–Xu 低直径聚类加简单修剪，O(log² n) 轮；确定版本通过白盒改造 Ghaffari–Grunau [FOCS 2024] 的递归网络分解算法，Õ(log² n) 轮。算法流程极简：边染色逆序处理各层并对每簇染色；局部平衡割正序处理并在每簇内计算局部最大割。

Result: 在 LOCAL 模型下：(1) 对任意常数 ε>0，得到 Õ(log² n) 轮确定性 (3/2+ε)Δ-边染色算法，适用于最大度 Δ≥Δ₀(ε) 的图，二分图对所有 Δ 成立；(2) 对任意 0<ε<1/4，得到 Õ(log² n) 轮确定性 (1/4-ε)-局部平衡割算法，其中每个顶点至少 (1/4-ε) 比例的邻居在另一侧。

Conclusion: 内向聚类保证使简单处理流程超越网络分解通常的贪心适用范围，为 LOCAL 模型下确定性边染色和局部平衡割提供了简洁高效的算法，并可通过随机与确定性两种方式构造分解。

Abstract: We introduce a graph decomposition primitive called introvert clustering, which strengthens standard low-diameter clustering by guaranteeing that every clustered vertex keeps at least a $\left(\frac12-\varepsilon\right)$-fraction of its relevant neighbors in its own cluster. Repeatedly applying this primitive yields a layered introvert network decomposition with $O(\log n)$ layers and weak diameter $O(\log n)$.
  We give two applications in the $\mathsf{LOCAL}$ model. For every constant $\varepsilon>0$, we obtain a $\widetilde O(\log^2 n)$-round deterministic algorithm for list $\left(\frac32+\varepsilon\right)Δ$-edge coloring on graphs of maximum degree $Δ\geqΔ_0(\varepsilon)$; for bipartite graphs, the result holds for all $Δ$. For every constant $0<\varepsilon<1/4$, we also obtain a $\widetilde O(\log^2 n)$-round deterministic algorithm for a $\left(\frac14-\varepsilon\right)$-locally balanced cut, where every vertex has at least a $\left(\frac14-\varepsilon\right)$-fraction of its neighbors on the opposite side.
  The resulting algorithms are remarkably simple: edge coloring processes the layers in reverse order and colors each cluster, while locally balanced cut processes them forward and computes a locally maximum cut within each cluster. The introvert guarantee enables these procedures beyond the usual greedy regime of network decomposition.
  We construct the decomposition in $O(\log^2 n)$ randomized rounds using Miller--Peng--Xu low-diameter clustering and a simple trimming procedure, and deterministically in $\widetilde O(\log^2 n)$ rounds via a white-box adaptation of the recursive network decomposition algorithm of Ghaffari and Grunau [FOCS 2024].

</details>


### [31] [CEDD-optimizer: Enabling Cost-Efficient Dataset Distillation on Geographically Distributed Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10151)
*Dai Liu,Eishi Arima,Martin Schulz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CEDD-optimizer框架，用于在非均匀边缘环境中实现成本高效的分布式数据集蒸馏（DD），在满足训练质量约束下最小化总成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式学习需要从分布式边缘设备收集数据并汇聚到中心主机训练，但数据收集的通信开销成为瓶颈。数据集蒸馏（DD）因高压缩比而有吸引力，但DD在非均匀边缘环境中的成本效率尚未被充分探索。地理分布的边缘设备面临不同的能源与数据传输价格，而DD超参数（如目标压缩比、蒸馏步数）显著影响能耗、传输开销和下游测试精度，因此需要在本地和全局两个层面进行调优。

Method: 提出成本高效数据集蒸馏优化器（CEDD-optimizer），一个面向成本高效分布式DD的超参数调优框架。它在训练质量约束下最小化总成本，通过优化各边缘设备的超参数设置并考虑环境非均匀性。框架含两个模块：CEDD-calibrator负责确定能耗模型与训练质量模型的参数，其中能耗模型通过离线校准确定，训练质量模型通过三步调优方案在线估计；CEDD-solver基于这些模型求解成本最小化问题，以引导和改进分布式DD工作流。

Result: 在多种图像数据集上的实验表明，在相同质量约束下，该方法相比基线DD方法最高取得20.8的提升。

Conclusion: CEDD-optimizer通过联合优化本地与全局超参数，在非均匀边缘环境下实现了成本高效的分布式数据集蒸馏，验证了DD在分布式数据上集中式学习的应用价值。

Abstract: Centralized learning is a fundamental paradigm in modern AI, in which data are collected from distributed edge devices and aggregated at a central host for model training. However, this pipeline is often bottlenecked by the substantial communication overhead of data collection. Dataset Distillation (DD), with its high compression ratio, is therefore attractive for centralized learning on distributed data. Yet, the cost efficiency of DD in non-uniform edge environments remains largely unexplored. Geographically distributed edge devices face different energy and data-transfer prices, while key DD hyperparameters, such as the target compression ratio and number of distillation steps, substantially affect energy use, transfer overhead, and downstream test accuracy. These hyperparameters must therefore be tuned both locally and globally. We propose the Cost-Efficient Dataset Distillation optimizer (CEDD-optimizer), a hyperparameter-tuning framework for cost-efficient distributed DD. It minimizes total cost under a training-quality constraint by optimizing hyperparameter settings across edge devices while accounting for environmental non-uniformity. The framework comprises two modules: CEDD-calibrator and CEDD-solver. CEDD-calibrator identifies parameters for our energy and training-quality models: the former is determined through offline calibration, whereas the latter is estimated online through a three-step tuning scheme. Based on these models, CEDD-solver solves the cost-minimization problem to steer and improve the distributed DD workflow. Experiments across various image datasets show that our approach achieves up to a 20.8 improvement over the baseline DD method under the same quality constraint.

</details>


### [32] [Stencil Computation at the Intersection of AI and HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10368)
*Timothee Ewart,Mauricio Araya-Polo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文将张量编译器（TinyTC 与 PyTorch/Triton）应用于8阶25点声学模板计算，在多种GPU上对比性能，发现该模板计算主要受带宽限制，编译器管理的L1/LSC缓存可有效替代手动共享内存分级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量编译器最初为AI工作负载设计，但其分块与内存抽象同样适用于科学和工业应用中的高阶模板计算。作者希望验证这一思路，并对硬件专用实现与可移植实现进行对比，以指导跨厂商HPC模板开发路径的选择。

Method: 针对大规模网格上的8阶25点声学模板（含边界条件），面向GPGPU实现两种方案：硬件专用的TinyTC实现，以及基于PyTorch/Triton的可移植实现。评估平台涵盖Intel B70、B580、GPU MAX 1550，NVIDIA A100/RTX6000 Blackwell/H100，以及AMD MI325x。采用roofline模型与内存层级profiling进行分析。

Result: 在Battlemage B580上，随机初始化下TinyTC达到15.6 Gpts/s，PT/Triton为13.5 Gpts/s；零初始化运行因硬件内存压缩最高可达35.8 Gpts/s。profiling表明性能主要受带宽限制，编译器管理的L1/LSC缓存可有效替代程序员手动管理的共享内存分级。

Conclusion: TinyTC是Intel硬件上面向性能的优选路径，而PyTorch/Triton则是跨厂商HPC模板开发中兼顾可移植性与生产力的强基线。

Abstract: Tensor compilers such as TinyTC and OpenAI Triton were originally developed for AI workloads, but the same tiling and memory abstractions can be applied to implement efficient high-order stencils for scientific and industrial applications. We demonstrate this for an 8th-order, 25-point acoustic stencil with boundary conditions over an a demanding-sized grid, targeting GPGPUs, where we compare the hardware-specialized TinyTC implementation with a portable PyTorch/Triton implementation. The target platforms for evaluation include Intel B70, B580, GPU MAX 1550, NVIDIA A100/RTX6000 Blackwell/H100, and AMD MI325x. For instance, on Battlemage B580 TinyTC reaches 15.6 Gpts/s versus 13.5 Gpts/s for PT/Triton under random initialization, while zero-initialized runs reach up to 35.8 Gpts/s due to hardware memory compression. Using roofline and memory-hierarchy profiling, we show that -as expected- performance is predominantly bandwidth-limited and that compiler-managed L1/LSC caching can effectively replace programmer-managed shared-memory staging for this stencil class. Overall, the results position TinyTC as the performance-oriented path on Intel hardware and PyTorch/Triton as a strong portability/productivity baseline for cross-vendor HPC stencil development.

</details>


### [33] [Avatar: Toward Autonomous End-to-End Orchestration of Scientific Workflows using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10509)
*Suman Raj,Hai Duc Nguyen,Haochen Pan,Ryan Chard,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Avatar是一个基于actor的工作流编排架构（编排器、执行器、溯源监控器），通过统一的动作目录使规则与LLM策略可插拔，在三个负载上验证：规则模式复现原生执行，LLM模式减少55%计算浪费、降低40% GPU忙碌时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流管理系统依赖固定、手工调优的规则进行编排，LLM智能体虽被寄予更自主编排的期望，但引入智能体推理的位置、风险边界以及实际收益仍不明确。

Method: 提出Avatar，基于actor的架构，包含编排器、执行器与溯源监控器；各actor的决策策略通过一个经适配器验证的动作目录实现可插拔（规则型或LLM驱动），使传统与智能体控制在同一核心上跨不同WMS运行；基于Academy框架实现并在三个负载上评估。

Result: 规则模式复现原生执行，且单一未修改核心可运行全部三个负载；LLM驱动的Avatar报告计算浪费减少55%，GPU忙碌时间缩短40%。

Conclusion: Avatar为工作流系统从遵循预设规则转向对自身编排进行推理提供了可行的一步。

Abstract: Scientific workflow management (WMSs) systems automate execution, yet orchestrate using fixed, hand-tuned rules. LLM agents promise more autonomous orchestration, but it remains unclear where to introduce agentic reasoning, how to bound its risk, and when it actually helps. We present Avatar, an actor-based architecture comprising an orchestrator, an executor, and a provenance monitor. Each actor's decision policy is pluggable (rule-based or LLM-backed) via a single adapter-validated action catalog, so conventional and agentic control run on the same core across different WMSs. We present an implementation using the Academy framework and evaluate Avatar across three workloads. We observe that Avatar's rule mode reproduces native execution, with a single unchanged core running all three. Moreover, LLM-backed Avatar reports a reduction of compute wastage by $55\%$ and cuts GPU-busy time by $40\%$. Overall, we envision Avatar as a step toward workflow systems that reason about their own orchestration rather than follow pre-fixed rules.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [34] [EEGBind: Detecting Source-Level Interictal Epileptiform Discharges via EEG-Centric Multimodal Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09728)
*Muchen Li,Anglin Liu,Xuetian Gao,Ruijian Xu,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEGBind，一种以EEG为中心的多模态绑定框架，用于五类源级IED分类，在NMM-Source-IED基准上加权F1达0.8395。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源级IED分析对术前评估与治疗规划很重要，但短EEG窗口中的源区证据微弱、不完整，且受被试差异、类别不平衡和多模态上下文不完善影响。任务不仅需检测IED是否存在，还需将其分配到临床意义明确的脑区类别。

Method: EEGBind以EEG为主模态，将同步视频上下文特征绑定到EEG中心表示上；不采用早期或过强的多模态融合以避免扰动源敏感EEG表示，而是把视频作为辅助证据；并引入视角一致修复阶段提升隐藏集鲁棒性，同时保持已学到的源类别边界。

Result: 在NeuroMM 2026 Grand Challenge Track 3 NMM-Source-IED基准上，加权F1达到0.8395，优于强竞争者。

Conclusion: 结果支持以EEG为中心的多模态绑定是源级IED分类的实用策略，代码已开源。
